[codex] # AGENTS.md Checkstyle
[codex] 
[codex] **Status:** Active
[codex] **Purpose:** Provide reusable rules for writing `AGENTS.md` files that help
[codex] AI coding agents work safely and accurately without creating documentation
[codex] drift.
[codex] 
[codex] Use this document as a public reference when creating or reviewing an
[codex] `AGENTS.md` file in any repository:
[codex] 
[codex] ```text
[codex] Inspect this repository and generate an AGENTS.md file using the guidelines in
[codex] budgetanalyzer/orchestration/docs/agents-md-checkstyle.md.
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] For remote use, point agents at:
[codex] https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration/blob/main/docs/agents-md-checkstyle.md
[codex] 
[codex] ## Core Standard
[codex] 
[codex] An `AGENTS.md` file is an operating contract for coding agents. It should teach
[codex] the agent how to work in the repository, where to find current truth, what it
[codex] may change, what it must not change, and how to verify work.
[codex] 
[codex] It should not become a stale inventory of services, classes, endpoints, ports,
[codex] dependency versions, or setup steps that already have a closer source of truth.
[codex] 
[codex] The durable pattern is:
[codex] 
[codex] ```text
[codex] Stable rule + discovery command + source-of-truth pointer
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Prefer this:
[codex] 
[codex] ````markdown
[codex] Service manifests live under kubernetes/services/. Discover them with:
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] find kubernetes/services -maxdepth 2 -type f | sort
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Exact ports and exposure rules live in docs/architecture/port-reference.md.
[codex] ````
[codex] 
[codex] Avoid this:
[codex] 
[codex] ```markdown
[codex] Services:
[codex] - transaction-service on port 8082
[codex] - currency-service on port 8084
[codex] - permission-service on port 8086
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] The first form survives refactoring. The second form drifts.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Preservation Rule
[codex] 
[codex] When tightening or refactoring an existing `AGENTS.md`, preserve unique
[codex] information. Do not remove a rule, guardrail, workflow, boundary, or operating
[codex] constraint unless one of these is true:
[codex] 
[codex] - The same meaning is preserved in the rewritten `AGENTS.md`.
[codex] - The information is moved to a clearly named, closer source-of-truth document
[codex]   and `AGENTS.md` now points to it.
[codex] - The rule is genuinely obsolete for the current repository, and the rewrite
[codex]   says so intentionally rather than deleting it silently.
[codex] 
[codex] Treat deletion as the last option, not the default cleanup action.
[codex] 
[codex] Do not equate "shorter" with "better." If a section contains non-duplicated
[codex] repo-specific guidance, keep it or re-home it before removing it.
[codex] 
[codex] Example:
[codex] 
[codex] ```markdown
[codex] Keep this kind of direct guardrail if no closer owner doc exists:
[codex] 
[codex] ## Git Safety
[codex] 
[codex] Never run git write commands such as `commit`, `push`, `checkout`, `reset`, or
[codex] branch manipulation unless explicitly requested.
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] If you replace a concrete section with a pointer, verify the pointer actually
[codex] owns the full instruction. Do not replace unique guidance with an empty link.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Consultation Trigger Pattern
[codex] 
[codex] Important pointers should explain when an agent should read the referenced
[codex] file. A path alone says where detail exists; a consultation trigger tells the
[codex] agent when to spend context on that detail.
[codex] 
[codex] The trigger does not need to be a literal `When to consult` block. It can be an
[codex] inline rule, a section-level bullet list, or a short imperative sentence. Use
[codex] the block form when several related triggers need to be grouped together.
[codex] 
[codex] Use this pattern for source-of-truth docs, config files, runbooks, generated
[codex] artifacts, and decision records:
[codex] 
[codex] ```text
[codex] Topic summary:
[codex] - Stable rule or short orientation.
[codex] - Current details live in &lt;path>.
[codex] - Trigger: read &lt;path> when &lt;specific task or failure mode applies>.
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Good forms:
[codex] 
[codex] ```markdown
[codex] Before writing or modifying Java code, read
[codex] docs/code-quality-standards.md. Do not skip this step.
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] ```markdown
[codex] For comprehensive testing strategies, read docs/testing-patterns.md when
[codex] writing tests.
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] ```markdown
[codex] API routes are resource-based and should stay decoupled from service names.
[codex] Current gateway routes live in nginx/nginx.k8s.conf.
[codex] 
[codex] When to consult nginx/nginx.k8s.conf:
[codex] - Adding or changing a public `/api/...` route.
[codex] - Debugging a request that reaches the SPA instead of a backend.
[codex] - Verifying local and production gateway parity.
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Avoid this:
[codex] 
[codex] ```markdown
[codex] See nginx/nginx.k8s.conf for routes.
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Good triggers are action-oriented:
[codex] 
[codex] - Before changing setup, read the getting-started or bootstrap docs.
[codex] - Before changing public APIs, read OpenAPI specs and route ownership docs.
[codex] - Before changing deployment, read manifests, overlays, and deployment runbooks.
[codex] - Before changing architecture or ownership, read the relevant ADRs and active
[codex]   architecture docs.
[codex] - When a verifier fails, read the focused troubleshooting runbook before
[codex]   inventing a workaround.
[codex] 
[codex] Do not add consultation triggers mechanically for every link. Use them for
[codex] references where timing matters or where agents are likely to guess instead of
[codex] loading the owner doc.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Imperative Language
[codex] 
[codex] Write agent instructions as direct operating rules, not passive descriptions.
[codex] Agents follow clear commands better than background prose.
[codex] 
[codex] Prefer imperative forms:
[codex] 
[codex] ```markdown
[codex] Before writing or modifying Java code, read docs/code-quality-standards.md.
[codex] Run `./gradlew test` before finishing Java changes.
[codex] Do not edit generated files directly.
[codex] Stop and report missing prerequisites instead of inventing workarounds.
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Avoid descriptive forms that leave the action implicit:
[codex] 
[codex] ```markdown
[codex] The code-quality standards document contains Java style guidance.
[codex] Tests are available through Gradle.
[codex] Generated files are usually produced by the build.
[codex] Prerequisites may be required.
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Use `must`, `never`, `always`, `do not`, `before`, `after`, and `when` where
[codex] the rule is mandatory. Use `prefer` where the guidance is a default with
[codex] legitimate exceptions.
[codex] 
[codex] Do not make every sentence forceful. Repository purpose, architecture summaries,
[codex] and source-of-truth descriptions can be explanatory. Guardrails, workflows,
[codex] validation gates, and consultation triggers should be imperative.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Rule Set
[codex] 
[codex] | ID | Rule | Check |
[codex] | --- | --- | --- |
[codex] | AG001 | Prefer patterns over inventories. | If adding a list of concrete services, classes, endpoints, ports, or versions, ask whether a command or owner doc can reveal it instead. |
[codex] | AG002 | Point to the closest source of truth. | Link to config, manifests, README files, runbooks, OpenAPI specs, package files, or build files instead of copying their exact detail. |
[codex] | AG003 | Include discovery commands. | Give `rg`, `find`, `tree`, package-manager, runtime, or framework commands that reveal current state. |
[codex] | AG004 | Keep stable guardrails directly in `AGENTS.md`. | Include write boundaries, destructive-command policy, security constraints, validation requirements, and workflow rules that agents must always see. |
[codex] | AG005 | Keep volatile details out of `AGENTS.md`. | Do not duplicate full setup flows, generated route maps, dependency tables, class inventories, or API contracts. |
[codex] | AG006 | Make repository authority explicit. | State the repo role, scope, read/write permissions, related repos, and ownership boundaries. |
[codex] | AG007 | Use prerequisite-first workflows. | Tell agents to check required tools, environment, credentials, and repo-owned setup docs before implementing work. |
[codex] | AG008 | Define verification gates. | Name the tests, linters, smoke checks, scripts, or manual checks expected before work is complete. |
[codex] | AG009 | Keep paths portable. | Use relative paths or repo-root-relative paths. Avoid machine-specific absolute paths. |
[codex] | AG010 | Avoid eager file imports unless intended. | Use plain text paths (`docs/file.md`) or markdown links (`[docs/file.md](docs/file.md)`). In Claude-oriented files, avoid `@path` syntax, which pre-loads content at startup. |
[codex] | AG011 | Use hierarchy intentionally. | Put global rules at the repo root and place subtree-specific `AGENTS.md` files near specialized code only when the agent tooling supports hierarchical context. |
[codex] | AG012 | Keep active docs separate from history. | Point to active owner docs for implementation work. Mark archives and decision records as context unless they are intentionally active surfaces. |
[codex] | AG013 | Preserve useful direct context. | Do not shrink `AGENTS.md` into a bare index. Keep enough high-signal rules for agents to act without rediscovering basic operating constraints. |
[codex] | AG014 | Make missing prerequisites explicit. | Tell agents to stop and report missing required tools or environment state rather than inventing workarounds. |
[codex] | AG015 | Explain when to update `AGENTS.md`. | Update it for new instructions, guardrails, workflows, or discovery commands, not for every ordinary code or config change. |
[codex] | AG016 | Add consultation triggers for important references. | For key docs and config pointers, name the concrete tasks or failure modes that should trigger reading them. Inline guidance is fine; a literal `When to consult` block is optional. |
[codex] | AG017 | Use imperative language for agent actions. | Write guardrails, workflows, validation gates, and consultation triggers as direct commands. Avoid passive descriptions when action is required. |
[codex] | AG018 | Re-home unique guidance before removing it. | Do not delete repo-specific instructions just to reduce length. Preserve them in `AGENTS.md`, or move them to a real owner doc and leave a pointer. |
[codex] 
[codex] ## Recommended Structure
[codex] 
[codex] Use the sections that fit the repository. Rename them if local terminology is
[codex] clearer, but keep the same intent.
[codex] 
[codex] ### 1. Repository Position
[codex] 
[codex] State what the repo is and what authority it has.
[codex] 
[codex] Include:
[codex] 
[codex] - Archetype, such as application, library, service, orchestration, tooling, or
[codex]   documentation.
[codex] - Scope, such as one service, one package, a monorepo area, or a whole
[codex]   ecosystem.
[codex] - Relationships to sibling repos, generated artifacts, deployment repos, or
[codex]   shared libraries.
[codex] - Read and write boundaries for agents.
[codex] 
[codex] Example:
[codex] 
[codex] ```markdown
[codex] ## Repository Position
[codex] 
[codex] **Archetype:** service
[codex] **Scope:** payments API
[codex] **Role:** owns payment authorization and capture workflows
[codex] 
[codex] ### Boundaries
[codex] - Read shared contracts from ../service-common.
[codex] - Write application code, tests, and docs in this repo.
[codex] - Do not change deployment manifests in ../orchestration from this context.
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] ### 2. Discovery
[codex] 
[codex] Give commands that reveal current structure and runtime state. Discovery
[codex] commands are better than static inventories because they remain accurate when
[codex] the repository changes.
[codex] 
[codex] Common examples:
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] # Repo structure
[codex] find . -maxdepth 2 -type f | sort
[codex] tree -L 2 -I 'node_modules|target|build|dist|.git'
[codex] 
[codex] # Text and symbol search
[codex] rg -n "pattern"
[codex] rg --files
[codex] 
[codex] # Build tool state
[codex] ./gradlew tasks
[codex] npm run
[codex] go test ./...
[codex] 
[codex] # Runtime state
[codex] docker compose ps
[codex] kubectl get pods -A
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Only include commands that are relevant to the repo and likely to work in a
[codex] normal development environment.
[codex] 
[codex] ### 3. Source Of Truth
[codex] 
[codex] Name canonical docs and files by topic. This lets `AGENTS.md` summarize without
[codex] copying. For high-value references, include consultation triggers so the agent
[codex] knows when to load the owner doc.
[codex] 
[codex] Good source-of-truth pointers include:
[codex] 
[codex] - Setup and onboarding docs.
[codex] - Architecture docs.
[codex] - API specs.
[codex] - Build files and dependency catalogs.
[codex] - Runtime manifests.
[codex] - Script catalogs.
[codex] - Runbooks.
[codex] - Security policy docs.
[codex] - ADRs for decision rationale.
[codex] 
[codex] Example:
[codex] 
[codex] ```markdown
[codex] ## Source Of Truth
[codex] 
[codex] - Local setup: docs/development/getting-started.md
[codex] - API contract: docs/api/openapi.yaml
[codex] - Build and dependency rules: build.gradle.kts and gradle/libs.versions.toml
[codex] - Deployment manifests: ../orchestration/kubernetes/services/payments/
[codex] - Operational runbooks: docs/runbooks/
[codex] 
[codex] Consultation triggers:
[codex] - Setup docs: before changing prerequisites, bootstrap, or local run behavior.
[codex] - API contracts: before adding, removing, or reshaping endpoints.
[codex] - Deployment manifests: before changing probes, ports, resources, or service
[codex]   wiring.
[codex] - Runbooks: when reproducing or debugging an operational failure.
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] ### 4. Operating Rules
[codex] 
[codex] Put stable constraints directly in `AGENTS.md`. Agents should not have to hunt
[codex] for rules that prevent unsafe or invalid changes.
[codex] 
[codex] Common operating rules:
[codex] 
[codex] - Never run destructive git commands unless explicitly requested.
[codex] - Do not commit, push, reset, or rewrite history unless explicitly requested.
[codex] - Do not write outside the allowed repository or owned subtree.
[codex] - Do not edit generated files directly unless the generator is unavailable and
[codex]   the limitation is documented.
[codex] - Do not bypass security, auth, policy, persistence, or validation layers as a
[codex]   durable fix.
[codex] - Treat manual live-environment changes as diagnostics unless they are encoded
[codex]   back into repo-owned config.
[codex] - Stop and report if a required prerequisite is missing.
[codex] 
[codex] Do not demote stable guardrails into a vague summary. If the repository has a
[codex] direct rule like "Never run git write commands unless explicitly requested,"
[codex] keep that exact operational meaning visible unless a closer owner doc now
[codex] authoritatively owns it.
[codex] 
[codex] ### 5. Development Workflow
[codex] 
[codex] Describe the normal path for making changes without duplicating long setup
[codex] docs.
[codex] 
[codex] Include:
[codex] 
[codex] - The prerequisite check or setup doc to read first.
[codex] - The default build, test, and local run entry points.
[codex] - The preferred debugging flow.
[codex] - The correct place to add new files or modules.
[codex] - The docs that must change with code changes.
[codex] 
[codex] Example:
[codex] 
[codex] ````markdown
[codex] ## Development Workflow
[codex] 
[codex] Read docs/development/getting-started.md before changing setup assumptions.
[codex] Use the checked-in scripts rather than reconstructing commands from memory.
[codex] 
[codex] Before finishing service code changes, run:
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] ./gradlew test
[codex] ./gradlew check
[codex] ```
[codex] ````
[codex] 
[codex] ### 6. Validation
[codex] 
[codex] Be explicit about completion gates. If validation differs by change type, say
[codex] so.
[codex] 
[codex] Example:
[codex] 
[codex] ```markdown
[codex] ## Validation
[codex] 
[codex] - Shell scripts: `bash -n &lt;script>` and `shellcheck &lt;script>`.
[codex] - Java changes: `./gradlew test` and `./gradlew check`.
[codex] - Frontend changes: `npm test` and `npm run lint`.
[codex] - Kubernetes changes: `kubectl apply --dry-run=server -f &lt;file>` when a
[codex]   cluster is available.
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Also say what to do when a gate cannot run:
[codex] 
[codex] ```markdown
[codex] If a required verifier cannot run because a tool, cluster, credential, or
[codex] service is unavailable, report that explicitly. Do not claim the work is fully
[codex] verified.
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] ### 7. Documentation Maintenance
[codex] 
[codex] Tell agents when to update docs and which docs own recurring topics.
[codex] 
[codex] Recommended rules:
[codex] 
[codex] - Update `AGENTS.md` when repo instructions, guardrails, discovery commands, or
[codex]   workflows change.
[codex] - Update `README.md` when setup, usage, public purpose, or human onboarding
[codex]   changes.
[codex] - Update `docs/` when architecture, operations, APIs, behavior, or design
[codex]   rationale changes.
[codex] - Do not update archived docs unless explicitly requested.
[codex] - Do not duplicate a detailed topic across multiple docs. Choose or update the
[codex]   owner doc and link to it.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Specificity Guidance
[codex] 
[codex] Use this table to decide what belongs directly in `AGENTS.md`.
[codex] 
[codex] | Content | Put In `AGENTS.md`? | Better Owner |
[codex] | --- | --- | --- |
[codex] | Repo role and scope | Yes | `AGENTS.md` |
[codex] | Agent write boundaries | Yes | `AGENTS.md` |
[codex] | Destructive command policy | Yes | `AGENTS.md` |
[codex] | Security or compliance guardrails | Yes | `AGENTS.md`, with links to detailed policy docs |
[codex] | Discovery commands | Yes | `AGENTS.md` |
[codex] | Full setup sequence | Usually no | Getting-started docs or setup scripts |
[codex] | Complete port table | No | Architecture docs, manifests, compose files |
[codex] | API endpoint inventory | No | OpenAPI specs, route files, controller discovery |
[codex] | Dependency versions | No | Build files, lockfiles, version catalogs |
[codex] | Class names and package inventories | No | Source code discovery |
[codex] | Historical rationale | Brief pointer only | ADRs or decision records |
[codex] | Generated output definitions | No | Generator docs or generated artifacts |
[codex] 
[codex] Rule of thumb:
[codex] 
[codex] ```text
[codex] If it changes during normal implementation, point to the source of truth.
[codex] If it constrains every agent action, keep it directly in AGENTS.md.
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] ## Anti-Patterns
[codex] 
[codex] Avoid these patterns during authoring and review.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Static Inventories
[codex] 
[codex] Static lists of services, endpoints, ports, packages, classes, or versions tend
[codex] to drift. Replace them with discovery commands and owner-doc links.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Duplicated Procedures
[codex] 
[codex] If a setup flow or operational runbook already exists, `AGENTS.md` should link
[codex] to it and summarize when to use it. It should not maintain a second copy.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Vague Guardrails
[codex] 
[codex] Rules like "be careful with deployment" are too weak. Name the exact forbidden
[codex] or required behavior.
[codex] 
[codex] Prefer:
[codex] 
[codex] ```markdown
[codex] Do not use `--address 0.0.0.0` for observability port-forwards.
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Avoid:
[codex] 
[codex] ```markdown
[codex] Be careful exposing observability.
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] ### Context Preload By Accident
[codex] 
[codex] Some agent tools support syntax that imports file contents into the startup
[codex] context. For example, in Claude-oriented context files, `@docs/file.md` imports
[codex] the file rather than acting as a lazy pointer.
[codex] 
[codex] For portable `AGENTS.md` files, prefer plain text paths or markdown links:
[codex] 
[codex] ```markdown
[codex] Read docs/architecture/system-overview.md when changing service boundaries.
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Use eager imports only when you intentionally want the full referenced content
[codex] loaded every time.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Token Minimization As The Only Goal
[codex] 
[codex] Short files are not automatically good. A useful `AGENTS.md` should be concise,
[codex] but it must still include high-signal operating constraints that agents need
[codex] before making changes.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Generation Workflow
[codex] 
[codex] When asking an agent to create a new `AGENTS.md`, require this workflow.
[codex] 
[codex] 1. Inspect the repository structure with `find`, `tree`, or equivalent
[codex]    commands.
[codex] 2. Read the primary human docs, such as `README.md`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`, setup
[codex]    docs, architecture docs, and script catalogs.
[codex] 3. Inspect build files, package files, manifests, and test configuration to
[codex]    discover current commands.
[codex] 4. Identify the repository archetype, ownership boundaries, and closest source
[codex]    of truth for recurring topics.
[codex] 5. Draft `AGENTS.md` using stable rules, discovery commands, and pointers
[codex]    instead of copied inventories.
[codex] 6. Add consultation triggers for references that agents should not ignore
[codex]    during related changes.
[codex] 7. Compare the draft against the existing `AGENTS.md` and explicitly preserve,
[codex]    re-home, or intentionally retire each repo-specific rule that is not
[codex]    duplicated elsewhere.
[codex] 8. Verify every included command is syntactically plausible and every referenced
[codex]    file exists.
[codex] 9. Update documentation indexes or ownership maps if the repository has them.
[codex] 
[codex] Do not generate `AGENTS.md` from assumptions alone. The file should be grounded
[codex] in discovered repository facts.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Review Checklist
[codex] 
[codex] Use this checklist before accepting a new or modified `AGENTS.md`.
[codex] 
[codex] - The repo role, scope, and agent write boundaries are explicit.
[codex] - The file contains discovery commands for structure, source search, and key
[codex]   runtime or build state.
[codex] - The file points to owner docs for setup, architecture, APIs, operations, and
[codex]   deployment instead of copying their full detail.
[codex] - Important references include inline or block-form triggers for the tasks or
[codex]   failures that require reading them.
[codex] - Guardrails, workflow steps, and validation gates use imperative language.
[codex] - Stable safety rules are directly visible.
[codex] - Unique repo-specific guidance from the previous `AGENTS.md` was preserved,
[codex]   re-homed to a real owner doc, or intentionally retired with justification.
[codex] - Validation gates are clear by change type.
[codex] - Missing-prerequisite behavior is explicit.
[codex] - No machine-specific absolute paths are required.
[codex] - No stale-looking inventories of services, ports, endpoints, classes, or
[codex]   dependency versions are maintained.
[codex] - Historical docs and ADRs are treated as context unless explicitly active.
[codex] - The file is long enough to be useful but not bloated with details owned
[codex]   elsewhere.
[codex] - References use plain paths or markdown links unless eager import semantics are
[codex]   intentional.
[codex] - No section was removed solely because it looked verbose. Information loss was
[codex]   checked explicitly.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Minimal Template
[codex] 
[codex] Use this as a starting point, then tailor it to the repository.
[codex] 
[codex] ````markdown
[codex] # &lt;Project> Agent Instructions
[codex] 
[codex] ## Repository Position
[codex] 
[codex] **Archetype:** &lt;application | service | library | orchestration | tooling>
[codex] **Scope:** &lt;owned system area>
[codex] **Role:** &lt;what this repo owns>
[codex] 
[codex] ### Boundaries
[codex] - Read: &lt;allowed external paths or systems>
[codex] - Write: &lt;owned paths>
[codex] - Do not write: &lt;forbidden paths or generated surfaces>
[codex] 
[codex] ## Discovery
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] # Repo structure
[codex] find . -maxdepth 2 -type f | sort
[codex] 
[codex] # Source search
[codex] rg -n "&lt;important pattern>"
[codex] 
[codex] # Available scripts or build tasks
[codex] &lt;repo-specific command>
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] ## Source Of Truth
[codex] 
[codex] - Setup: &lt;path>
[codex] - Architecture: &lt;path>
[codex] - API contracts: &lt;path>
[codex] - Build and dependencies: &lt;path>
[codex] - Operations: &lt;path>
[codex] 
[codex] Consultation triggers:
[codex] - &lt;path>: &lt;trigger for reading this owner doc>.
[codex] - &lt;path>: &lt;trigger for reading this owner doc>.
[codex] - &lt;path>: &lt;trigger for reading this owner doc>.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Operating Rules
[codex] 
[codex] - Do not &lt;forbidden action>.
[codex] - Always &lt;required action>.
[codex] - Before &lt;task>, read &lt;path>.
[codex] - Stop and report &lt;missing prerequisite or boundary issue>.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Development Workflow
[codex] 
[codex] - Check prerequisites in &lt;path> before implementation.
[codex] - Use &lt;command> for the normal local run path.
[codex] - Follow &lt;path> when adding new modules, routes, or services.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Validation
[codex] 
[codex] - &lt;change type>: &lt;command>
[codex] - &lt;change type>: &lt;command>
[codex] 
[codex] If a required verifier cannot run, report the missing prerequisite or failure
[codex] instead of claiming full verification.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Documentation Maintenance
[codex] 
[codex] - Update `AGENTS.md` when agent instructions or guardrails change.
[codex] - Update `README.md` when human setup or usage changes.
[codex] - Update `docs/` when architecture, behavior, operations, or APIs change.
[codex] - Keep detailed recurring topics in one owner doc and link to it here.
[codex] ````
[codex] 
[codex] ## Relationship To Decision Records
[codex] 
[codex] This checkstyle is distilled from the pattern-based documentation principles in
[codex] docs/decisions/003-pattern-based-claude-md.md. That decision record remains
[codex] historical context and rationale. This file is the active reusable authoring
[codex] standard for new `AGENTS.md` files.
[codex] 
[codex] exec
[codex] /bin/bash -lc "sed -n '1,700p' Tiltfile" in /workspace/orchestration
[codex]  succeeded in 0ms:
[codex] # ============================================================================
[codex] # Budget Analyzer - Tiltfile
[codex] # Complete local development environment with live reload
[codex] # ============================================================================
[codex] 
[codex] # Load extensions
[codex] load('ext://restart_process', 'docker_build_with_restart')
[codex] load('ext://uibutton', 'cmd_button', 'location')
[codex] load('ext://configmap', 'configmap_create')
[codex] load('ext://dotenv', 'dotenv')
[codex]

[codex] # Helper function to encode values to base64
[codex] def shell_single_quote(value):
[codex]     """Quote a string for safe single-quoted shell usage."""
[codex]     return "'" + value.replace("'", "'\"'\"'") + "'"
[codex] 
[codex] def encode_secret_data(data):
[codex]     """Encode dictionary values to base64 for Kubernetes secrets."""
[codex]     encoded = {}
[codex]     for k, v in data.items():
[codex]         encoded[k] = str(local("printf %s " + shell_single_quote(v) + " | base64 | tr -d '\\n'", quiet=True)).strip()
[codex]     return encoded
[codex] 
[codex] def json_escape(value):
[codex]     """Escape a string for safe JSON interpolation."""
[codex]     escaped = value.replace('\\', '\\\\')
[codex]     escaped = escaped.replace('"', '\\"')
[codex]     escaped = escaped.replace('\b', '\\b')
[codex]     escaped = escaped.replace('\f', '\\f')
[codex]     escaped = escaped.replace('\n', '\\n')
[codex]     escaped = escaped.replace('\r', '\\r')
[codex]     escaped = escaped.replace('\t', '\\t')
[codex]     return escaped
[codex] 
[codex] def json_string(value):
[codex]     """Render a JSON string literal."""
[codex]     return '"' + json_escape(value) + '"'
[codex] 
[codex] def build_rabbitmq_definitions(admin_user, admin_password, service_user, service_password, vhost):
[codex]     """Build RabbitMQ definitions JSON for local boot-time import."""
[codex]     return """{
[codex]   "users": [
[codex]     {
[codex]       "name": %s,
[codex]       "password": %s,
[codex]       "tags": ["administrator"]
[codex]     },
[codex]     {
[codex]       "name": %s,
[codex]       "password": %s,
[codex]       "tags": []
[codex]     }
[codex]   ],
[codex]   "vhosts": [
[codex]     {
[codex]       "name": %s
[codex]     }
[codex]   ],
[codex]   "permissions": [
[codex]     {
[codex]       "user": %s,
[codex]       "vhost": %s,
[codex]       "configure": ".*",
[codex]       "write": ".*",
[codex]       "read": ".*"
[codex]     },
[codex]     {
[codex]       "user": %s,
[codex]       "vhost": %s,
[codex]       "configure": "^(amq\\\\.gen.*|exchange-rate\\\\.import\\\\.requested|exchange-rate\\\\.import\\\\.requested\\\\.exchange-rate-import-service(\\\\.dlq)?|DLX)$",
[codex]       "write": "^(amq\\\\.default|exchange-rate\\\\.import\\\\.requested|exchange-rate\\\\.import\\\\.requested\\\\.exchange-rate-import-service(\\\\.dlq)?|DLX)$",
[codex]       "read": "^(exchange-rate\\\\.import\\\\.requested|exchange-rate\\\\.import\\\\.requested\\\\.exchange-rate-import-service(\\\\.dlq)?|DLX)$"
[codex]     }
[codex]   ]
[codex] }""" % (
[codex]         json_string(admin_user),
[codex]         json_string(admin_password),
[codex]         json_string(service_user),
[codex]         json_string(service_password),
[codex]         json_string(vhost),
[codex]         json_string(admin_user),
[codex]         json_string(vhost),
[codex]         json_string(service_user),
[codex]         json_string(vhost),
[codex]     )
[codex] 
[codex] def secret_blob(name, namespace, data):
[codex]     """Render a Kubernetes Secret as a blob."""
[codex]     encoded = encode_secret_data(data)
[codex]     lines = [
[codex]         'apiVersion: v1',
[codex]         'kind: Secret',
[codex]         'metadata:',
[codex]         '  name: ' + name,
[codex]         '  namespace: ' + namespace,
[codex]         'type: Opaque',
[codex]         'data:',
[codex]     ]
[codex]     for key in encoded:
[codex]         lines.append('  ' + key + ': ' + encoded[key])
[codex]     return blob('\n'.join(lines) + '\n')
[codex] 
[codex] def create_secret(name, namespace, data):
[codex]     """Apply a Kubernetes Secret generated by Tilt."""
[codex]     k8s_yaml(secret_blob(name, namespace, data))
[codex] 
[codex] def configmap_blob(name, namespace, data):
[codex]     """Render a Kubernetes ConfigMap as a blob."""
[codex]     lines = [
[codex]         'apiVersion: v1',
[codex]         'kind: ConfigMap',
[codex]         'metadata:',
[codex]         '  name: ' + name,
[codex]         '  namespace: ' + namespace,
[codex]         'data:',
[codex]     ]
[codex]     for key, value in data.items():
[codex]         lines.append('  ' + key + ': ' + json_string(str(value)))
[codex]     return blob('\n'.join(lines) + '\n')
[codex] 
[codex] def create_configmap(name, namespace, data):
[codex]     """Apply a Kubernetes ConfigMap generated by Tilt."""
[codex]     k8s_yaml(configmap_blob(name, namespace, data))
[codex]

[codex] local("""
[codex] set -eu
[codex] mkdir -p .tilt/api-docs
[codex] python3 - &lt;&lt;'PY'
[codex] import json
[codex] import subprocess
[codex] from pathlib import Path
[codex] 
[codex] root = Path.cwd()
[codex] parent = root.parent
[codex] source_path = root / "kubernetes/production/docs-aggregator/release-metadata.json"
[codex] output_path = root / ".tilt/api-docs/release-metadata.json"

[codex] def git(repo, *args):
[codex]     result = subprocess.run(
[codex]         ["git", "-C", str(repo), *args],
[codex]         check=False,
[codex]         stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
[codex]         stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
[codex]         text=True,
[codex]     )
[codex]     if result.returncode != 0:
[codex]         return ""
[codex]     return result.stdout.strip()
[codex] 
[codex] 
[codex] def local_source_state(artifact):
[codex]     source_repo = artifact.get("sourceRepository", "")
[codex]     repo = parent / source_repo
[codex]     manifest_ref = artifact.get("sourceRef", "")
[codex]     manifest_commit = artifact.get("sourceCommit", "")
[codex]     manifest_tag = manifest_ref.removeprefix("refs/tags/")
[codex] 
[codex]     if not repo.is_dir():
[codex]         return {
[codex]             "status": "missing-repo",
[codex]             "label": "missing repo",
[codex]             "dirty": False,
[codex]             "manifestTag": manifest_tag,
[codex]             "matchesManifestTag": False,
[codex]         }
[codex] 
[codex]     head_commit = git(repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD")
[codex]     dirty = bool(git(repo, "status", "--porcelain", "--untracked-files=normal"))
[codex]     head_tags = git(repo, "tag", "--points-at", "HEAD").splitlines()
[codex]     head_tag = head_tags[0] if head_tags else ""
[codex]     manifest_tag_commit = ""
[codex] 
[codex]     if manifest_tag:
[codex]         manifest_tag_commit = git(repo, "rev-parse", "--verify", f"refs/tags/{manifest_tag}^{{}}")
[codex] 
[codex]     matches_manifest_tag = bool(
[codex]         head_commit
[codex]         and manifest_tag_commit
[codex]         and head_commit == manifest_tag_commit
[codex]         and (not manifest_commit or head_commit == manifest_commit)
[codex]     )
[codex] 
[codex]     if dirty and matches_manifest_tag:
[codex]         status = "modified"
[codex]         label = "modified from manifest tag"
[codex]     elif dirty:
[codex]         status = "modified"
[codex]         label = "modified local head"
[codex]     elif matches_manifest_tag:
[codex]         status = "matches-manifest-tag"
[codex]         label = "matches manifest tag"
[codex]     elif head_commit and manifest_commit and head_commit == manifest_commit:
[codex]         status = "matches-manifest-commit"
[codex]         label = "matches manifest commit"
[codex]     elif head_tag:
[codex]         status = "different-tag"
[codex]         label = f"different tag {head_tag}"
[codex]     elif head_commit:
[codex]         status = "untagged"
[codex]         label = "untagged local head"
[codex]     else:
[codex]         status = "unknown"
[codex]         label = "unknown"
[codex] 
[codex]     return {
[codex]         "status": status,
[codex]         "label": label,
[codex]         "dirty": dirty,
[codex]         "headCommit": head_commit,
[codex]         "headTag": head_tag,
[codex]         "manifestTag": manifest_tag,
[codex]         "matchesManifestTag": matches_manifest_tag,
[codex]     }
[codex] 
[codex] 
[codex] with source_path.open(encoding="utf-8") as source:
[codex]     metadata = json.load(source)
[codex] 
[codex] deployment = metadata.setdefault("deployment", {})
[codex] deployment["productionBaselineId"] = deployment.get("id", "")
[codex] deployment["id"] = "tilt-local-development"
[codex] deployment["environment"] = "local-development"
[codex] deployment["runtime"] = "tilt"
[codex] 
[codex] for artifact in metadata.get("artifacts", {}).values():
[codex]     artifact["localSource"] = local_source_state(artifact)
[codex] 
[codex] rendered = json.dumps(metadata, indent=2) + "\\n"
[codex] if not output_path.exists() or output_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") != rendered:
[codex]     output_path.write_text(rendered, encoding="utf-8")
[codex] PY
[codex] """, quiet=True)
[codex]

[codex] def spring_gradle_deps(repo_path):
[codex]     """Tracked Gradle inputs that can affect dependency resolution or build output."""
[codex]     return [
[codex]         repo_path + '/src',
[codex]         repo_path + '/build.gradle.kts',
[codex]         repo_path + '/settings.gradle.kts',
[codex]         repo_path + '/gradle/libs.versions.toml',
[codex]         repo_path + '/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties',
[codex]     ]
[codex] 
[codex] def service_common_publish_deps(repo_path):
[codex]     """Tracked service-common inputs that can affect published Maven Local artifacts."""
[codex]     return [
[codex]         repo_path + '/build.gradle.kts',
[codex]         repo_path + '/settings.gradle.kts',
[codex]         repo_path + '/gradle/libs.versions.toml',
[codex]         repo_path + '/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties',
[codex]         repo_path + '/service-core/src',
[codex]         repo_path + '/service-core/build.gradle.kts',
[codex]         repo_path + '/service-web/src',
[codex]         repo_path + '/service-web/build.gradle.kts',
[codex]         repo_path + '/spring-platform/build.gradle.kts',
[codex]         repo_path + '/spring-cloud-platform/build.gradle.kts',
[codex]     ]
[codex] 
[codex] def spring_boot_dockerfile(port):
[codex]     """Inline dev Dockerfile that selects the executable Spring Boot Jar."""
[codex]     return '''
[codex] # renovate: datasource=docker depName=eclipse-temurin
[codex] FROM eclipse-temurin:25-jre-alpine@sha256:c707c0d18cb9e8556380719f80d96a7529d0746fbb42143893949b98ed2f8943
[codex] WORKDIR /app
[codex] RUN addgroup -g 1001 -S appgroup && adduser -u 1001 -S appuser -G appgroup
[codex] COPY build/libs/*.jar /tmp/libs/
[codex] RUN set -eu; \\
[codex]     jar="$(find /tmp/libs -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '*.jar' ! -name '*-plain.jar' | sort | head -n 1)"; \\
[codex]     test -n "$jar"; \\
[codex]     cp "$jar" /app/app.jar; \\
[codex]     rm -rf /tmp/libs; \\
[codex]     chown -R appuser:appgroup /app
[codex] USER appuser
[codex] EXPOSE ''' + str(port) + '''
[codex] '''
[codex] 
[codex] def spring_boot_live_update(repo_path):
[codex]     """Live update the executable Jar after Gradle rebuilds it locally."""
[codex]     return [
[codex]         sync(repo_path + '/build/libs', '/tmp/libs'),
[codex]         run(
[codex]             'set -eu; jar="$(find /tmp/libs -maxdepth 1 -type f -name \'*.jar\' ! -name \'*-plain.jar\' | sort | head -n 1)"; test -n "$jar"; cp "$jar" /app/app.jar',
[codex]             trigger=[repo_path + '/build/libs'],
[codex]         ),
[codex]     ]
[codex]

[codex] # Publish service-common to Maven Local when it changes
[codex] # All backend services depend on this
[codex] local_resource(
[codex]     'service-common-publish',
[codex]     cmd='cd ' + get_repo_path('service-common') + ' && ./gradlew publishToMavenLocal --parallel --build-cache && ' +
[codex]         'tilt trigger transaction-service-compile && ' +
[codex]         'tilt trigger currency-service-compile && ' +
[codex]         'tilt trigger permission-service-compile && ' +
[codex]         'tilt trigger session-gateway-compile',
[codex]     deps=service_common_publish_deps(get_repo_path('service-common')),
[codex]     resource_deps=['kind-node-inotify-budget'],
[codex]     labels=['compile'],
[codex]     allow_parallel=True,
[codex]     auto_init=True
[codex] )

[codex] # ============================================================================
[codex] # SPRING BOOT SERVICE BUILD PATTERN
[codex] # ============================================================================
[codex] 
[codex] def spring_boot_service(name, deps=[]):
[codex]     """
[codex]     Build pattern for Spring Boot services with live reload.
[codex] 
[codex]     Args:
[codex]         name: Service name (must match repository name)
[codex]         deps: Additional resource dependencies
[codex]     """
[codex]     repo_path = get_repo_path(name)
[codex]     port = SERVICE_PORTS[name]
[codex]     debug_port = DEBUG_PORTS.get(name)
[codex] 
[codex]     # Step 1: Local Gradle compilation
[codex]     local_resource(
[codex]         name + '-compile',
[codex]         cmd='cd ' + repo_path + ' && ./gradlew bootJar --parallel --build-cache -x test',
[codex]         deps=spring_gradle_deps(repo_path),
[codex]         resource_deps=['service-common-publish'],
[codex]         labels=['compile'],
[codex]         allow_parallel=True,
[codex]         auto_init=True
[codex]     )
[codex] 
[codex]     # Step 2: Docker build with restart capability
[codex]     # Use inline dev Dockerfile that copies pre-built JAR (avoids Maven dependency issues)
[codex]     docker_build_with_restart(
[codex]         name,
[codex]         context=repo_path,
[codex]         dockerfile_contents=spring_boot_dockerfile(port),
[codex]         entrypoint=[
[codex]             'java',
[codex]             '-jar',
[codex]             '/app/app.jar'
[codex]         ],
[codex]         live_update=spring_boot_live_update(repo_path)
[codex]     )
[codex] 
[codex]     # Step 3: Load Kubernetes manifests
[codex]     service_manifests = [
[codex]         'kubernetes/services/' + name + '/serviceaccount.yaml',
[codex]         'kubernetes/services/' + name + '/deployment.yaml',
[codex]         'kubernetes/services/' + name + '/service.yaml',
[codex]     ]
[codex]     optional_configmap = 'kubernetes/services/' + name + '/configmap.yaml'
[codex]     resource_objects = [
[codex]         name + ':serviceaccount',
[codex]         name + '-postgresql-credentials:secret',
[codex]     ]
[codex]     if os.path.exists(optional_configmap):
[codex]         service_manifests.append(optional_configmap)
[codex]         resource_objects.append(name + '-config:configmap')
[codex]     if name == 'currency-service':
[codex]         resource_objects.extend([
[codex]             'currency-service-rabbitmq-credentials:secret',
[codex]             'currency-service-redis-credentials:secret',
[codex]             'fred-api-credentials:secret',
[codex]         ])
[codex]     if name == 'transaction-service':
[codex]         resource_objects.append('transaction-service-preview-import-token-credentials:secret')
[codex]     k8s_yaml(service_manifests)
[codex] 
[codex]     # Step 4: Configure resource with port forwards and dependencies
[codex]     port_forwards_list = [
[codex]         port_forward(port, port, name='HTTP'),
[codex]     ]
[codex]     if debug_port:
[codex]         port_forwards_list.append(port_forward(debug_port, 5005, name='Debug'))
[codex] 
[codex]     base_deps = ['postgresql', 'rabbitmq', 'istio-injection'] if name == 'currency-service' else ['postgresql', 'istio-injection'] if name in ['transaction-service', 'permission-service'] else ['istio-injection']
[codex] 
[codex]     k8s_resource(
[codex]         name,
[codex]         objects=resource_objects,
[codex]         port_forwards=port_forwards_list,
[codex]         labels=['backend'] if name in ['transaction-service', 'currency-service', 'permission-service'] else ['gateway'],
[codex]         resource_deps=[name + '-compile'] + base_deps + deps,
[codex]     )
[codex] 
[codex] # ============================================================================
[codex] # BACKEND MICROSERVICES
[codex] # ============================================================================
[codex] 
[codex] # Transaction Service
[codex] spring_boot_service('transaction-service')
[codex] 
[codex] # Currency Service
[codex] spring_boot_service('currency-service')
[codex] 
[codex] # Permission Service
[codex] spring_boot_service('permission-service')

[codex] 
[codex] def frontend_smoke_image_build_command(repo_path, dockerfile):
[codex]     """Build the local production-smoke bundle and its image as one operation."""
[codex]     return (
[codex]         'cd ' + shell_single_quote(repo_path) + ' && '
[codex]         + 'if [ ! -d node_modules ]; then '
[codex]         + "printf '%s\\n' " + shell_single_quote(
[codex]             'The budget-analyzer-web-prod-smoke image build requires local npm dependencies in the sibling budget-analyzer-web repo. Run "npm install" there before re-running Tilt. This preflight only applies to the local /_prod-smoke/ build path; the normal frontend pod still installs its dependencies inside its image.'
[codex]         ) + ' >&2; '
[codex]         + 'exit 1; '
[codex]         + 'fi && '
[codex]         + 'npm run build:prod-smoke && '
[codex]         + 'docker build --file ' + shell_single_quote(dockerfile)
[codex]         + ' --tag "$EXPECTED_REF" dist'
[codex]     )
[codex] 
[codex] def frontend_smoke_build_deps(repo_path):
[codex]     """Tracked sibling inputs for the local /_prod-smoke/ bundle."""
[codex]     return [
[codex]         repo_path + '/src',
[codex]         repo_path + '/public',
[codex]         repo_path + '/index.html',
[codex]         repo_path + '/package.json',
[codex]         repo_path + '/package-lock.json',
[codex]         repo_path + '/postcss.config.js',
[codex]         repo_path + '/tailwind.config.js',
[codex]         repo_path + '/tsconfig.json',
[codex]         repo_path + '/tsconfig.node.json',
[codex]         repo_path + '/vite.config.ts',
[codex]         repo_path + '/.env',
[codex]         repo_path + '/.env.local',
[codex]         repo_path + '/.env.production',
[codex]         repo_path + '/.env.production.local',
[codex]     ]
[codex] 
[codex] # Build the production-smoke bundle and its asset image atomically so Tilt only
[codex] # updates NGINX after both steps succeed.
[codex] custom_build(
[codex]     'budget-analyzer-web-prod-smoke',
[codex]     command=frontend_smoke_image_build_command(frontend_repo, frontend_smoke_dockerfile),
[codex]     deps=frontend_smoke_build_deps(frontend_repo) + [frontend_smoke_dockerfile],
[codex] )
[codex]

[codex] # Existing default namespace pods need one reinjection after the Istio CNI cutover.
[codex] # New pods created after the cutover should already land without istio-init.
[codex] local_resource(
[codex]     'istio-reinject-default-workloads',
[codex]     cmd='''
[codex]         restart_required=false
[codex]         for deployment in budget-analyzer-web currency-service ext-authz nginx-gateway permission-service session-gateway transaction-service; do
[codex]             if ! kubectl get deployment "$deployment" -n default >/dev/null 2>&1; then
[codex]                 continue
[codex]             fi
[codex] 
[codex]             init_containers=$(kubectl get pods -n default -l "app=${deployment}" \
[codex]                 -o jsonpath='{range .items[*]}{.spec.initContainers[*].name}{"\n"}{end}' 2>/dev/null || true)
[codex] 
[codex]             if printf '%s\n' "$init_containers" | grep -qw 'istio-init'; then
[codex]                 kubectl rollout restart "deployment/${deployment}" -n default
[codex]                 restart_required=true
[codex]             fi
[codex]         done
[codex] 
[codex]         if [ "$restart_required" = true ]; then
[codex]             for deployment in budget-analyzer-web currency-service ext-authz nginx-gateway permission-service session-gateway transaction-service; do
[codex]                 if kubectl get deployment "$deployment" -n default >/dev/null 2>&1; then
[codex]                     kubectl rollout status "deployment/${deployment}" -n default --timeout=180s
[codex]                 fi
[codex]             done
[codex]         else
[codex]             echo "Default namespace deployments are already running without istio-init"
[codex]         fi
[codex]     ''',
[codex]     resource_deps=[
[codex]         'budget-analyzer-web',
[codex]         'currency-service',
[codex]         'ext-authz',
[codex]         'nginx-gateway',
[codex]         'permission-service',
[codex]         'session-gateway',
[codex]         'transaction-service',
[codex]         'istio-cni',
[codex]     ],
[codex]     labels=['infrastructure'],
[codex] )
[codex]

[codex] # Namespace labeling for sidecar injection and Pod Security Admission

[codex]         kubectl label namespace default pod-security.kubernetes.io/enforce=restricted --overwrite
[codex]         kubectl label namespace default pod-security.kubernetes.io/enforce-version=v1.32 --overwrite
[codex]         kubectl label namespace default pod-security.kubernetes.io/warn=restricted --overwrite
[codex]         kubectl label namespace default pod-security.kubernetes.io/warn-version=v1.32 --overwrite
[codex]         kubectl label namespace default pod-security.kubernetes.io/audit=restricted --overwrite
[codex]         kubectl label namespace default pod-security.kubernetes.io/audit-version=v1.32 --overwrite
[codex]         kubectl label namespace infrastructure istio-injection=disabled --overwrite
[codex]         kubectl label namespace infrastructure pod-security.kubernetes.io/enforce=baseline --overwrite
[codex]         kubectl label namespace infrastructure pod-security.kubernetes.io/enforce-version=v1.32 --overwrite
[codex]         kubectl label namespace infrastructure pod-security.kubernetes.io/warn=baseline --overwrite
[codex]         kubectl label namespace infrastructure pod-security.kubernetes.io/warn-version=v1.32 --overwrite
[codex]         kubectl label namespace infrastructure pod-security.kubernetes.io/audit=baseline --overwrite
[codex]         kubectl label namespace infrastructure pod-security.kubernetes.io/audit-version=v1.32 --overwrite
[codex]         kubectl label namespace istio-system pod-security.kubernetes.io/enforce=privileged --overwrite
[codex]         kubectl label namespace istio-system pod-security.kubernetes.io/enforce-version=v1.32 --overwrite
[codex]         kubectl label namespace istio-system pod-security.kubernetes.io/warn=privileged --overwrite
[codex]         kubectl label namespace istio-system pod-security.kubernetes.io/warn-version=v1.32 --overwrite
[codex]         kubectl label namespace istio-system pod-security.kubernetes.io/audit=privileged --overwrite
[codex]         kubectl label namespace istio-system pod-security.kubernetes.io/audit-version=v1.32 --overwrite
[codex]     ''',
[codex]     resource_deps=['istio-tracing-telemetry', 'infrastructure-namespace'],
[codex]     labels=['infrastructure'],
[codex] )
[codex] 
[codex] # Istio security policies (PeerAuthentication + AuthorizationPolicy)
[codex] # Explicitly delete PERMISSIVE PeerAuthentication resources removed during the Istio ingress migration.
[codex] # kubectl apply does not delete resources removed from a multi-document YAML file.
[codex] local_resource(
[codex]     'istio-security-policies',
[codex]     cmd='''
[codex]         kubectl delete peerauthentication nginx-gateway-permissive ext-authz-permissive session-gateway-permissive -n default --ignore-not-found
[codex]         kubectl apply -f kubernetes/istio/peer-authentication.yaml
[codex]         kubectl apply -f kubernetes/istio/authorization-policies.yaml
[codex]     ''',
[codex]     deps=[
[codex]         'kubernetes/istio/peer-authentication.yaml',
[codex]         'kubernetes/istio/authorization-policies.yaml',

[codex]             exit 1
[codex]         fi

[codex]                 -n istio-egress --ignore-not-found

[codex] name: Dependency Automation Configuration
[codex] 
[codex] on:
[codex]   push:
[codex]     branches: [main]
[codex]     paths:
[codex]       - '.github/workflows/dependency-automation-config.yml'
[codex]       - 'renovate.json'
[codex]       - 'renovate-presets/**'
[codex]   pull_request:
[codex]     branches: [main]
[codex]     paths:
[codex]       - '.github/workflows/dependency-automation-config.yml'
[codex]       - 'renovate.json'
[codex]       - 'renovate-presets/**'
[codex]   workflow_dispatch:
[codex]     inputs:
[codex]       run_hosted_dry_run:
[codex]         description: Run the post-publication GitHub-platform full dry run
[codex]         required: true
[codex]         default: false
[codex]         type: boolean
[codex] 
[codex] permissions:
[codex]   contents: read
[codex] 
[codex] env:
[codex]   FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex]   # renovate: datasource=npm depName=renovate
[codex]   RENOVATE_VERSION: '44.65.5'
[codex] 
[codex] jobs:
[codex]   validate-renovate-config:
[codex]     runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] 
[codex]     steps:
[codex]       - name: Checkout code
[codex]         uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] 
[codex]       - name: Set up Node.js
[codex]         uses: actions/setup-node@v6
[codex]         with:
[codex]           node-version: '24'
[codex] 
[codex]       - name: Validate repository configuration and shared preset
[codex]         run: >-
[codex]           npx --yes "renovate@${RENOVATE_VERSION}" renovate-config-validator
[codex]           --strict --no-global renovate.json renovate-presets/default.json
[codex] 
[codex]   hosted-renovate-dry-run:
[codex]     if: ${{ github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' && inputs.run_hosted_dry_run }}
[codex]     needs: validate-renovate-config
[codex]     permissions:
[codex]       checks: read
[codex]       contents: read
[codex]       issues: read
[codex]       pull-requests: read
[codex]       security-events: read
[codex]       statuses: read
[codex]     runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] 
[codex]     steps:
[codex]       - name: Set up Node.js
[codex]         uses: actions/setup-node@v6
[codex]         with:
[codex]           node-version: '24'
[codex] 
[codex]       - name: Run read-only hosted full dry run
[codex]         env:
[codex]           LOG_LEVEL: debug
[codex]           RENOVATE_ONBOARDING: 'false'
[codex]           RENOVATE_REQUIRE_CONFIG: required
[codex]           RENOVATE_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
[codex]         run: >-
[codex]           npx --yes "renovate@${RENOVATE_VERSION}"
[codex]           --platform=github
[codex]           --dry-run=full
[codex]           "${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}"
[codex] # Dependency Automation
[codex] 
[codex] **Status:** Configuration prepared; administrator activation is still required.
[codex] 
[codex] This document owns the operating policy for dependency automation across the
[codex] Budget Analyzer repositories. The preserved
[codex] [September 6 dependency review](research/dependency-update-review-2026-09-06.md)
[codex] is the historical acceptance benchmark; its observed versions are not bot
[codex] targets. The executable rollout and acceptance sequence remains in the
[codex] [dependency automation plan](plans/dependency-automation-plan.md).
[codex] 
[codex] ## Ownership and policy
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate is the only service allowed to open dependency update pull requests.
[codex] Dependabot remains alert-only: keep the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts
[codex] enabled, disable Dependabot version updates and overlapping Dependabot security
[codex] update pull requests, and let Renovate consume alerts when its App permission
[codex] allows that. Never enable automerge, including for security fixes.
[codex] 
[codex] The shared policy is
[codex] [`renovate-presets/default.json`](../renovate-presets/default.json). Repository
[codex] configuration extends it and adds only repository-specific manager paths and
[codex] extraction rules. Routine pull requests are created weekly, with no more than
[codex] three open routine pull requests and two new routine pull requests per hour in
[codex] one repository. Vulnerability fixes use Renovate's dedicated alert path and an
[codex] unrestricted schedule. Major, chart, stateful, platform, and checksum-coupled
[codex] updates require Dependency Dashboard approval.
[codex] 
[codex] Patch, minor, and major proposals remain distinct. Digest refreshes must retain
[codex] an immutable digest and any image flavor suffix. Reviewers must independently
[codex] verify Linux ARM64 support. An available tag or a changed digest is not evidence
[codex] of architecture support, embedded package versions, vulnerability remediation,
[codex] or upstream support status.
[codex] 
[codex] The following stay outside Renovate ownership:
[codex] 
[codex] - historical/generated outputs and the retained stale test suites
[codex] - local `:latest` and generated `:tilt-*` images listed by the image-pinning
[codex]   inventories
[codex] - first-party production images and their synchronized manifest, inventory,
[codex]   overlay, runtime metadata, and API-docs metadata
[codex] - Pod Security compatibility labels
[codex] - arbitrary per-platform checksum calculation
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate may propose checksum-coupled tool versions, but dashboard approval and
[codex] the existing checksum verification remain mandatory. A reviewer must update the
[codex] complete checksum table; no validation may be weakened to merge the proposal.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Cost and service boundary
[codex] 
[codex] Use the open-source Renovate engine through the free Mend Renovate Community
[codex] GitHub App. Do not provision a self-hosted bot or opt into paid Merge Confidence,
[codex] enterprise scheduling, enhanced cache, a trial, or another subscription. The
[codex] selected security evidence uses GitHub's dependency graph and Dependabot alerts,
[codex] Gradle's open-source basic dependency-submission path, Trivy, `npm audit`, and
[codex] `govulncheck`.
[codex] 
[codex] GitHub Actions usage is separate from Mend hosting. Public repositories can use
[codex] standard hosted Linux runners. Before enabling automation for a private
[codex] repository, confirm its included Actions allowance and that paid overages are
[codex] disabled. Stop for user direction if required coverage needs spending or a
[codex] capability unavailable on the free offering.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Activation procedure
[codex] 
[codex] Configuration preparation does not authorize GitHub setting changes. A
[codex] repository administrator performs activation only after all rollout phases have
[codex] prepared and validated their files:
[codex] 
[codex] 1. Publish the orchestration repository and its shared preset before publishing
[codex]    consumer configurations.
[codex] 2. At the start of Phase 12, manually dispatch
[codex]    `dependency-automation-config.yml` with `run_hosted_dry_run` enabled. Its
[codex]    GitHub-provided job token has read permissions only, Renovate runs with
[codex]    `--dry-run=full`, and the user retains control of the trigger. Preserve the
[codex]    workflow URL and confirm that the published preset resolves before proceeding;
[codex]    never copy the job token into an agent environment.
[codex] 3. Confirm repository visibility, Actions billing/overage settings, and that the
[codex]    free Mend service supports every scoped repository and required authenticated
[codex]    Maven lookup within its job limits.
[codex] 4. Install the Renovate Community App only on the repositories listed in the
[codex]    rollout plan. Grant read access to Dependabot alerts when available.
[codex] 5. Enable the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts. Disable Dependabot version
[codex]    updates and automatic Dependabot security-update pull requests.
[codex] 6. Configure private Maven access only through Mend-supported encrypted secrets
[codex]    and host rules. Never commit a token and never use `pull_request_target` to
[codex]    expose trusted credentials to dependency branches.
[codex] 7. Run onboarding for orchestration first. Inspect the resolved configuration,
[codex]    extraction logs, dashboard, job duration, and any rate-limit or timeout
[codex]    message before enabling consumers.
[codex] 8. Record every baseline item in the coverage report as reproduced, superseded,
[codex]    false positive with evidence, or missing. Update discovery, advisory evidence,
[codex]    and lifecycle assessment are separate results.
[codex] 
[codex] The Community service's documented single concurrent organization job,
[codex] approximately four-hour scheduling, and 30-minute timeout are acceptable only if
[codex] the pilot completes in practice. A timeout or unsupported authenticated lookup
[codex] blocks broader activation; it is not a reason to omit dependencies.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Local validation and dry run
[codex] 
[codex] The focused workflow validates both JSON files with Renovate's official
[codex] `renovate-config-validator` under Node 24. Before publishing the preset, run the
[codex] same strict schema validation locally. For an unpublished-preset dry run, load
[codex] `renovate-presets/default.json` as the local self-hosted configuration, suppress
[codex] repository config discovery, and process the checkout with the local platform.
[codex] Use debug logging and do not provide a GitHub token or enable a write-capable
[codex] platform mode. Local mode proves extraction and supported lookups but does not
[codex] perform a true full dry run. Phase 12 owns the post-publication GitHub-platform
[codex] full dry run through the manually dispatched, read-only workflow; its ephemeral
[codex] job token is never provided to an agent.
[codex] 
[codex] Extraction and lookup evidence belongs in
[codex] [`docs/research/dependency-automation-coverage.md`](research/dependency-automation-coverage.md),
[codex] not in a second desired-version inventory.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Failure triage
[codex] 
[codex] - **Configuration error:** reproduce with the pinned validator version. Treat
[codex]   warnings and migrations as failures; correct the preset or repository config.
[codex] - **No extraction:** check the owning file's native manager pattern or adjacent
[codex]   annotation and compare debug logs. Do not seed the historical expected version
[codex]   into configuration.
[codex] - **No lookup or wrong package:** verify the real datasource identity, chart
[codex]   name, registry URL, tag prefix, build suffix, and flavor suffix. Record an
[codex]   unresolved standard-tool gap instead of adding a custom crawler.
[codex] - **Authentication or resolution failure:** preserve the failure. Fix encrypted
[codex]   Maven/registry access in the supported trust boundary; do not use Maven Local,
[codex]   drop internal dependencies, or swallow the error.
[codex] - **Finding without an update:** retain the Dependabot/scanner finding and route
[codex]   inherited or transitive remediation to the owning service. An update pull
[codex]   request is not proof that an advisory was detected.
[codex] - **CI failure on a bot branch:** determine whether the proposal needs its
[codex]   documented checksum, chart rendering, ARM64, state migration, or cross-repo
[codex]   companion review. Do not relax a guardrail or create orchestration drift.
[codex] - **Scanner database/download failure:** report the job as failed separately
[codex]   from vulnerability findings. Never turn an unavailable scan into a clean result.
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate discovery and vulnerability scanners do not comprehensively establish
[codex] end-of-life status. Owners must still perform a quarterly support-policy review
[codex] against upstream lifecycle sources.
[codex] # Dependency Automation Coverage
[codex] 
[codex] **Status:** Orchestration extraction pilot complete; hosted observation and
[codex] security-scanner phases remain pending.  
[codex] **Last updated:** 2026-09-06
[codex] 
[codex] This report records observed extraction and lookup behavior. It is not a list
[codex] of desired dependency versions. The preserved
[codex] [dependency review](dependency-update-review-2026-09-06.md) remains the
[codex] historical acceptance benchmark, and
[codex] [Dependency Automation](../dependency-automation.md) owns operating policy.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Pilot evidence
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate `44.65.5` was run under Node `24.13.0` against a plain copy of the
[codex] checkout. The unpublished shared preset and repository configuration were
[codex] merged in memory, repository config discovery was suppressed, and local
[codex] platform dry-run mode was used with debug logging. This avoids resolving the
[codex] not-yet-published GitHub preset and includes newly created, untracked files.
[codex] 
[codex] Observed extraction totals were 95 occurrences in 34 package files:
[codex] 
[codex] | Manager | Files | Occurrences | Evidence |
[codex] | --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
[codex] | Dockerfile | 1 | 1 | `nginx/Dockerfile.prod-smoke-assets` -> `alpine` |
[codex] | GitHub Actions | 3 | 8 | Both existing workflows plus the new validator workflow; checkout, setup-node, Node input, and runner identities were recognized. |
[codex] | Helm values | 2 | 13 | Kyverno digest overrides and five kube-prometheus-stack image identities were recognized. |
[codex] | Kubernetes | 11 | 29 | Stateful infrastructure, Jaeger, service init images, gateway images, local first-party images, and Kubernetes API kinds were recognized. |
[codex] | Regex | 17 | 44 | Shell/Tilt chart pins, tool releases, Kind, Kiali, inline Temurin, probe images, Playwright, and the Renovate validator were recognized. |
[codex] 
[codex] These are the recorded initial-pilot totals. The subsequently added manual
[codex] hosted-dry-run job repeats the already-covered `actions/setup-node` identity; it
[codex] does not add a new dependency surface.
[codex] 
[codex] The first lookup run completed in about 34 seconds and reported 57 distinct
[codex] dependency records, 31 outdated records, and 104 candidate branches before
[codex] routine limits and dashboard approvals. Live lookups succeeded for all six
[codex] configured Helm repositories, npm, and most container registries. A repeat run
[codex] was interrupted by anonymous registry timeouts; that failure is retained below
[codex] instead of being presented as a clean scan.
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate's documented local platform supports `extract` and `lookup`, not a
[codex] true `full` dry run. Passing `--dry-run=full` falls back to lookup behavior.
[codex] Therefore extraction and live lookup are demonstrated locally, but branch-file
[codex] mutation and the published preset resolution still require a no-write hosted
[codex] observation after publication. No GitHub token was available in this worker,
[codex] by design, so GitHub-backed identities were extracted with
[codex] `github-token-required` and were not looked up. Those lookups are deferred to
[codex] the user-triggered Phase 12 job, which receives its ephemeral read-only token
[codex] inside GitHub Actions.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Orchestration extraction map
[codex] 
[codex] | Surface and source paths | Extracted identities | Lookup evidence or gap |
[codex] | --- | --- | --- |
[codex] | Production chart contract: `deploy/scripts/lib/version-contract.sh` | `base`, `external-secrets`, `cert-manager`, `kyverno`, `kube-prometheus-stack`, `kiali-server`; real Helm repository URL attached to each | All six repositories returned release metadata. Istio showed maintained-line and 1.30 proposals; later lines were also visible for the other charts. Every chart proposal is approval-gated. |
[codex] | Local chart commands: `Tiltfile` | Istio `base`, `cni`, `istiod`, and `gateway`; `kyverno`, `kube-prometheus-stack`, `kiali-server` | Lookups succeeded and repeated declarations resolve to the same datasource identities without native-manager duplication. Istio declarations are grouped per repository. |
[codex] | Production platform releases: `deploy/scripts/lib/version-contract.sh` | `k3s-io/k3s`, `kubernetes-sigs/gateway-api` | Extracted, including the literal `v` prefix and K3s `+k3sN` build component. Authenticated lookup is deferred to the Phase 12 hosted job. `PHASE4_POD_SECURITY_VERSION` is intentionally not extracted. |
[codex] | Checksum-coupled tools: `scripts/lib/pinned-tool-versions.sh` | `kubernetes/kubernetes`, `helm/helm`, `tilt-dev/tilt`, `FiloSottile/mkcert`, `kubernetes-sigs/kind`, `yannh/kubeconform`, `stackrox/kube-linter`, `kyverno/kyverno`, `kubernetes-sigs/gateway-api`, `projectcalico/calico` | All identities extracted; Tilt's absent `v` and Kyverno CLI's release prefix are explicit. Authenticated lookup is deferred to the Phase 12 hosted job. Proposals are approval-gated and state that Renovate cannot regenerate the checksum table. |
[codex] | Kind node: `kind-cluster-config.yaml` | `kindest/node:v1.35.0` plus its current digest | Docker lookup succeeded and exposed separate 1.35, 1.36, and 1.37 lines. Platform/ARM64 review is approval-gated. The stale setup-flow Kind image is excluded. |
[codex] | Stateful services: `kubernetes/infrastructure/{postgresql,rabbitmq,redis}/statefulset.yaml` and annotated probe scripts listed in `scripts/lib/image-pinning-targets.txt` | `postgres:16-alpine`, `rabbitmq:3.13-management`, `redis:7-alpine`, plus exact digests | Redis lookup exposed a maintained broad-tag digest refresh and Redis 8; RabbitMQ exposed RabbitMQ 4. PostgreSQL lookup timed out in the pilot. All are approval-gated. Broad tags do not identify the embedded Redis/PostgreSQL patch, so Redis 7.4.11 parity is **missing** until a separately reviewed explicit alias or image inventory proves it. |
[codex] | Edge and utility images: service manifests, `nginx/Dockerfile.prod-smoke-assets`, and annotated smoke scripts | `nginxinc/nginx-unprivileged`, `swaggerapi/swagger-ui`, `busybox`, `alpine`, `curlimages/curl`, `mendhak/http-https-echo`, `python` | Lookups exposed later NGINX maintained/minor lines, Swagger UI lines, and updates for the probe images. Exact digest refreshes were resolved. Repeated probe refs share identities. |
[codex] | Runtime JDK: inline Dockerfile in `Tiltfile` | `eclipse-temurin:25-jre-alpine` plus digest | Digest lookup succeeded, but the broad tag exposes only digest movement and not the embedded JDK patch. JDK patch/support parity is an explicit **gap** pending image inventory/scanning. |
[codex] | Jaeger: `kubernetes/monitoring/jaeger/deployment.yaml` | `cr.jaegertracing.io/jaegertracing/jaeger:2.17.0` plus digest | Lookup exposed 2.18, 2.19, and 2.20 lines. The matching literal in `scripts/guardrails/verify-production-image-overlay.sh` remains an intentional validation companion; Renovate does not silently rewrite that assertion. |
[codex] | Monitoring image overrides: `kubernetes/monitoring/prometheus-stack-values.yaml` | `docker.io/grafana/grafana`, `registry.k8s.io/kube-state-metrics/kube-state-metrics`, `quay.io/prometheus/prometheus`, and both Prometheus Operator images | Native Helm-values extraction found all five tags. Kube-state-metrics, Prometheus, and Operator lookups produced later lines; Grafana and config-reloader had registry timeouts on repeat validation. Rendering and exact-image scanning must close this **gap** in the later security-evidence phase. |

[codex] ## Phase 12: Observe activation and compare against the saved review
[codex] 
[codex] ### Workspace
[codex] 
[codex] .
[codex] 
[codex] ### Goal
[codex] 
[codex] Establish what the enabled automation independently detects and report remaining gaps.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Scope
[codex] 
[codex] Read-only hosted dry-run and activation evidence, orchestration coverage report,
[codex] and canonical docs.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Non-goals
[codex] 
[codex] No agent-performed GitHub settings changes, dependency merges, upgrades, sibling
[codex] fixes or deployment.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Required context
[codex] 
[codex] Read all phase evidence and the saved review. Prerequisite: the user has published
[codex] the shared preset and repo configs. Existing Maven credentials and zero-spend CI
[codex] settings must be verified. Local dry runs do not satisfy the hosted dry-run or
[codex] activation evidence. The user owns the manual workflow dispatch and administrator
[codex] activation steps; no credential is copied into the agent environment.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Execution steps
[codex] 
[codex] 1. Manually dispatch `dependency-automation-config.yml` with
[codex]    `run_hosted_dry_run` enabled. The job must use only GitHub's short-lived job
[codex]    token with explicit read permissions plus Renovate `--dry-run=full`. Preserve
[codex]    the workflow URL, verify that the published preset resolves, and record GitHub
[codex]    lookups, branch-file mutation simulation, registry retries, and explicit gaps.
[codex]    The user triggers this run; do not provide its token to an agent.
[codex] 2. Complete the administrator activation procedure in
[codex]    `docs/dependency-automation.md`, then inspect each repository's actual
[codex]    extraction/lookup logs, Dependency Dashboard,
[codex]    PR proposals, graph submissions, Dependabot alerts and scanner runs. Record run
[codex]    timestamps, config revisions, database versions and links. Observe at least two
[codex]    successful bot cycles and one scheduled scan cycle, using a manual first scan
[codex]    for immediate feedback. Missing authentication or rate-limit failures are gaps.
[codex] 3. Complete the Markdown comparison for every review area and named high-priority
[codex]    advisory. Separate exact matches, newer applicable recommendations, additional
[codex]    findings, applicability differences, deferred migrations and genuine misses.
[codex]    Do not require obsolete exact target versions or equal vulnerability counts.
[codex]    Record the user-applied workspace Node 24 prerequisite as baseline context, not
[codex]    as an update independently discovered or applied by Renovate.
[codex] 4. Check that maintained-line patches remain visible alongside major proposals,
[codex]    including Redis/PostgreSQL/Temurin digest changes, Spring inherited findings,
[codex]    controller images, frontend transitive packages and reachable go-redis findings.
[codex] 5. Demonstrate no duplicate update-PR ownership, no automerge, functioning bot PR
[codex]    checks and credential access, no paid features and no unintended production
[codex]    artifact promotion. Record free-service timing/resource failures if observed.
[codex] 6. Update the canonical guide with the verified operator workflow: weekly dashboard
[codex]    and alert review, scan-failure triage, quarterly upstream support-policy review,
[codex]    and adding a dependency through native extraction or a standard annotation.
[codex]    Correct active documentation drift only in its owner docs. Leave the archived
[codex]    notification guide, saved research and ADRs unchanged.
[codex] 7. Report automation installation and benchmark parity separately. Return any
[codex]    sibling configuration issue to its owning phase/context; do not fix sibling
[codex]    service logic or write a custom workaround from orchestration.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Implementation notes
[codex] 
[codex] Do not edit this executable plan while a runner invocation is using its snapshot;
[codex] write evidence to the coverage report. Actual security remediation, checksum
[codex] refreshes and major migrations happen in subsequent reviewed dependency work.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Validation
[codex] 
[codex] Check every baseline row has evidence and a disposition, every active repo has
[codex] successful runs, and all high-priority misses are prominently stated. Check links,
[codex] the read-only hosted dry-run URL, published-preset resolution, the zero-spend
[codex] settings evidence and that the historical review is unchanged.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Completion criteria
[codex] 
[codex] The free automation is demonstrably active across the scoped repos, standard
[codex] reports and update proposals are accessible, and the comparison states exactly
[codex] which findings were reproduced. Full review parity is claimed only when all
[codex] high-priority update/security findings are reproduced or superseded with evidence;
[codex] otherwise explicitly report partial coverage and the standard-tool limitations
[codex] for the user's decision. Lifecycle and exploitability assessment remain human work.
[codex] 
[codex] `service-common` is a library release line, not the stack version. Release it
[codex] when `spring-platform`, `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, or
[codex] `service-web` changes. Do not bump it only to ship a service image, route
[codex] change, documentation metadata fix, or production overlay update.
[codex] 
[codex] Tag helpers remain useful for human source management and rare named releases,
[codex] but tag names are not production correctness. OCI apply and verification use
[codex] the checked-in desired state and immutable image digests.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Orchestration Workflows
[codex] 
[codex] Dependency update pull-request ownership, activation, cost constraints, and
[codex] failure triage live in [Dependency Automation](dependency-automation.md). The
[codex] focused `dependency-automation-config.yml` workflow runs Renovate's official
[codex] strict configuration validator against the repository config and shared preset;
[codex] it does not install the App or open dependency pull requests.
[codex] 
[codex] ### `security-guardrails.yml`
[codex] 
[codex] The orchestration repo now has a dedicated static security workflow for the
[codex] manifest guardrail suite. It is intentionally additive beside `test-setup.yml`;
[codex] it does not reuse the stale DinD suites as current security guardrails.
[codex] 
[codex] It now runs on every push to `main` and every pull request targeting `main`.
[codex] The narrower path filter was removed so guarded security surfaces cannot bypass
[codex] the workflow when a new file path is added to the static scope.
[codex] 
[codex] What it runs:
[codex] 
[codex] - `./scripts/guardrails/verify-static-security-manifests.sh`
[codex] - `./scripts/guardrails/verify-static-security-manifests.sh --self-test`
[codex] 
[codex] The workflow bootstraps repo-pinned `helm`, `kubectl`, `kubeconform`,
[codex] `kube-linter`, and `kyverno` binaries through
[codex] `scripts/bootstrap/install-verified-tool.sh`, then runs:
[codex] 
[codex] - schema validation for checked-in manifests, with explicit missing-schema
[codex]   allowances for checked-in CRD resources such as Istio, Gateway API, Kyverno,
[codex]   and Prometheus Operator `ServiceMonitor` objects
[codex] - rendered Kustomize schema validation for the production app and
[codex]   infrastructure overlays, so partial patch files stay excluded from
[codex]   standalone validation but are still checked after render; the schema-v2
[codex]   production deployment manifest is also excluded because it is desired-state
[codex]   metadata, not a Kubernetes resource
[codex] - repo-specific kube-linter checks with documented exceptions
[codex] - Kyverno CLI pass/fail fixtures
[codex] - a generated Kyverno replay for representative approved local Tilt
[codex]   `:tilt-&lt;hash>` deploy refs derived from the checked-in contract inventory
[codex] - a rendered Kyverno production-chart check that rejects mutable controller and
[codex]   hook image refs from `deploy/helm-values/kyverno.values.yaml`
[codex] - pattern scans for image pinning, namespace PSA labels, and lingering
[codex]   pipe-to-shell guidance in active setup docs/scripts
[codex] 
[codex] That directory split is intentional: CI-safe checks live under
[codex] `scripts/guardrails/`, shared installer/bootstrap helpers live under
[codex] `scripts/bootstrap/`, and the live-cluster proof remains in
[codex] `scripts/smoketest/` so GitHub Actions cannot silently drift into a
[codex] static-only substitute for the local runtime gate.
[codex] 
[codex] Use the same command locally to reproduce workflow failures without a cluster:
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] ./scripts/guardrails/verify-static-security-manifests.sh
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] For full local security guardrail proof on a live cluster, use the separate
[codex] final gate:
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] ./scripts/smoketest/verify-security-guardrails.sh
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] That wrapper intentionally stays out of GitHub Actions because the runtime
[codex] proof depends on a live local cluster. CI for the security guardrails remains
[codex] static-only.
[codex]

[codex] usage() {
[codex]     cat &lt;&lt;'EOF'
[codex] Usage: scripts/guardrails/verify-static-security-manifests.sh [--self-test]
[codex] 
[codex] Runs the static security guardrail suite:
[codex] - kubeconform schema validation for checked-in manifests
[codex] - kubeconform schema validation for rendered production Kustomize overlays
[codex] - kube-linter with the repo-specific security baseline
[codex] - Kyverno CLI tests for the admission fixtures
[codex] - generated Kyverno replay for representative approved local Tilt deploy refs
[codex] - production Kyverno Helm render check for digest-pinned controller/hook images
[codex] - Tilt secret/config boundary inventory validation
[codex] - pattern scans for image pinning, namespace PSA labels, and pipe-to-shell guidance
[codex] 
[codex] Use --self-test to assert that the intentional failing fixtures are rejected.
[codex] EOF
[codex] }

<phase-complete>Configured the orchestration Renovate pilot: shared preset, root config, dependency annotations, official validator workflow, canonical policy/ownership links, and coverage report. Corrected the workflow’s npx invocation so the validator binary runs explicitly. Validation passed under Node 24.20.0: strict validation of both configs, local extraction/lookup (98 occurrences across 34 files; 101 candidate branches), actionlint, bash -n, ShellCheck, diff checks, static security guardrails, and guardrail self-tests. Dependency versions and digests remain unchanged. Policy preserves digest pinning, first-party release ownership, ARM64/checksum review, approval gates, and unrestricted security scheduling. GitHub-authenticated lookups, published-preset resolution, hosted full dry-run evidence, scanner evidence, and anonymous Docker-token retry gaps remain explicitly deferred to later phases. Handoff references: docs/dependency-automation.md and docs/research/dependency-automation-coverage.md.</phase-complete>
